## KnowledgeOS Reference Architecture v1.1 — DDD Bounded Context and Core Domain Model (2026-08-22, this session)

**Position:** the HPA review step of Reference Architecture v1.0, executed as a **DDD-based architectural refinement**. Research phase CLOSED (`20260822-1028`) → Reference Architecture v1.0 (PROPOSED, pending review) → **this review = DDD refinement → Reference Architecture v1.1** → Logical Architecture → Implementation Architecture → Systems. *"The next step should not be more extraction. The next step should be: DDD-based architectural refinement of KnowledgeOS Reference Architecture v1.0."*

**Commissioning instrument recorded:** `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1402-KOS-EP01-Reference-Architecture-v1.1-DDD-Refinement-commissioning-prompt.md` — the HPA's full DDD refinement commission (role: Principal Domain Architect + KnowledgeOS Chief Architect · objective: the smallest coherent architecture still truthfully called KnowledgeOS · forbidden actions: no tables/tech/APIs/classes/code/philosophy · quality gates: Reduction/Identity/Failure).

**Artifact produced:** `docs/knowledgeos/architecture/20260822-1402-KOS-EP01-Reference-Architecture-v1.1-DDD-Bounded-Context-and-Core-Domain-Model.md` — PROPOSED · EVIDENCE-BASED · NON-AUTHORITATIVE.

### The refinement's decisions

| Decision | Ruling |
|---|---|
| **Core domain** | **Knowledge Identity** — the domain of identity-bearing justified epistemic states and their justified life. Candidate cores tested: Knowledge Identity **ADOPTED** (removal → documents/embeddings/similarity = Ch IV refusals) · Epistemic Evolution **FOLDED IN** (the aggregate's lifecycle, not a second domain) · Meaning Preservation **FOLDED IN** (identity is identity *of* meaning) · Wisdom Formation **REJECTED** (not constitutional — no register row; V.3 forbids new concepts; future-gated behind amendment discipline) |
| **Bounded context map** | One **CORE** context (KnowledgeCore) · three **SUPPORTING** (Authority · Projection · Decision Boundary) · two **GENERIC/EXTERNAL** mechanism contexts (Reasoning & Validation engines · Expression). Hexagonal: every arrow into the core is a port; every arrow out is advisory or projection |
| **Aggregates** | **KnowledgeAggregate** (root: Knowledge; KnowledgeId assigned never derived) — members: Meaning · ContextTuple · EvidenceLinks · JustificationPath · Authority-ref · Agency · EpistemicState · TemporalValidity · Confidence (governed, never a scalar) · History · Relations. **ConflictRecord** (second aggregate — Art 8). Three small supporting aggregates: AuthorityGrant · DerivedView · DecisionRecord |
| **Invariants** | The eleven articles → **eleven aggregate invariants INV-KOS-IDENTITY-001 … INV-KOS-HISTORY-001**, enforcement locus moved from architectural boundary to the aggregate's command surface. The HPA's four example invariants all present (Representation≠Identity · Evidence≠Authority · Revision≠erase-History · Similarity≠Equality) |
| **Dimensions** | KEEP/MODIFY/REMOVE with removal test: nine dimensions → **seven protected aggregate members** + two processes externalized (reasoning-process → engines at the Verification Port; transformation → the aggregate's lifecycle/events) |
| **Semantic Compiler** | **NOT promoted** — candidate adapter at the Expression↔Meaning port, for the Logical Architecture stage (BV-8: do not promote without evidence) |
| **LLM** | = Language Cortex — external adapter at the Expression port; does not own truth; the two structural absences (identity · history) are placement (owned by the core) |
| **Zero** | **Meta-principle** — not an entity/aggregate/service/database object; sits above the architecture; realized through the aggregate's initial UNKNOWN state |

### Quality gates (all passed)

1. **Reduction Test** ✅ — eleven kernel services → one core domain + one primary aggregate; nine dimensions → seven members + two externalized processes.
2. **Identity Test** ✅ — the core is implementation-free; changing all implementations leaves it KnowledgeOS.
3. **Failure Test** ✅ — remove identity-of-meaning → the aggregate becomes a claim-store (Ch IV refusal).

### Discipline confirmations

- **Satisfies, never extends the Constitution** — the eleven invariants are renders of the frozen articles; no law added, none weakened.
- **Register 25+4 unchanged** · Kernel/Constitution/Character NO CHANGE · research closure unchanged.
- **Forbidden actions honored** — no database tables · no technologies · no APIs · no classes · no code · no new philosophy. The aggregates/value objects/events are named domain concepts with responsibility + invariant, not implementation.
- **`WisdomDerived`** — the HPA's example domain event — **recorded for traceability, NOT admitted** (wisdom is not a constitutional concept).

### Next (frozen sequence)

**HPA review of Reference Architecture v1.1** → **Logical Architecture** (the Trustworthy AI Engine — LLM + Semantic Compiler + KnowledgeOS — is named material for that stage, BV-8) → **Implementation Architecture** → **Systems**. Turing pending as the HPA-stated next lens, NOT executed.

## HPA review of v1.1 — PASS CONDITIONALLY — one wording refinement applied (2026-08-22, this session)

**Act:** The HPA delivered the v1.1 review verdict — **PASS CONDITIONALLY** (*"I would PASS the v1.1 DDD refinement conditionally. Not 'approved as final architecture' yet."*). Recorded verbatim-in-substance at `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1425-KOS-EP01-Reference-Architecture-v1.1-DDD-Refinement-HPA-Review.md`. The v1.1 artifact was revised (r2, same commit) to apply the review's one condition.

**What the HPA affirmed (nine assessments):** (1) the core decision is strong — `KnowledgeOS = identity-bearing epistemic state + justified lifecycle`; (2) the KEEP/MODIFY/REMOVE dimension purification — *"probably the most important DDD purification"*; (3) the KnowledgeAggregate — `REPRESENTATION ≠ MEANING ≠ IDENTITY`, KnowledgeId assigned never derived; (4) the Semantic Compiler decision — **NOT promoted**, ~74.2% accuracy = insufficient semantic accuracy → Logical Architecture investigation; (5) the clean separation of intelligence (LLM = Language Cortex · Semantic Compiler = candidate meaning intelligence · KnowledgeOS = epistemic core); (6) **one challenge — the condition**; (7) the domain events in place, the WisdomDerived non-admission discipline praised as demonstrating the architecture obeys the frozen Constitution; (8) the Gödel connection now architectural — states first-class (KNOWN/UNKNOWN/ABSENT/FALSE/QUESTIONABLE/REJECTED/CONFLICTED/VALIDATED), *"a semantic compiler may propose meaning; it may never manufacture epistemic status"*; (9) Sanskrit now has a specific architectural home (Pāṇinian-inspired semantic compilation → candidate mechanism at the Expression↔Meaning Port → Semantic AST/KIR → KnowledgeCore; investigate, not constitutionalize).

**The one condition (verbatim requested phrasing):** *"The KnowledgeAggregate is the authoritative domain boundary at which constitutional admissibility of a state transition is determined."* — replacing the implementation-flavored *"command surface"* / *"every command that changes a knowledge state is validated against the aggregate's invariants"* so v1.1 stays a Reference Architecture and does not pre-decide an implementation pattern; the concrete realization (commands · methods · domain services · policy evaluation) is a **Logical / Implementation Architecture decision, deliberately deferred**. **APPLIED** in v1.1 r2 at §1 · §3.1 · §3.3 · §4.1 · §6 (+ Altitude note) · §7.1 · Final Quality Gates; CONTEXT v1.1 block updated to match.

**Central review question answered (YES):** *"Is Knowledge Identity + justified lifecycle truly the smallest irreducible domain that remains when every mechanism, representation, technology, language, database, LLM, and reasoning engine is removed?"* — answered in v1.1 §3.3: remove identity → claim-store; remove lifecycle → snapshot-store; both Ch IV refusals. Nothing larger, nothing smaller.

**What the review does NOT decide:** not final approval (v1.1 remains PROPOSED · EVIDENCE-BASED · NON-AUTHORITATIVE) · no Semantic Compiler promotion · register **25+4 unchanged** · Constitution unchanged (FROZEN, satisfied never extended) · research not reopened (CLOSED).

**Next:** ⏳ **HPA confirmation of the applied wording** → the v1.1 review closes → **Logical Architecture** (Trustworthy AI Engine = named material for that stage, BV-8) → Implementation Architecture → Systems. Turing pending as the HPA-stated next lens, NOT executed.

## Second Architectural Review of v1.1 — Semantic Invariance & Compiler Boundary — PASS · CLARIFICATION ONLY (2026-08-22, this session)

**Act:** HPA commissioned an architect-side **second review** of the existing v1.1 — explicitly *not* another architecture-generation exercise (*"We should not commission another v1.1 architecture from scratch"*). Delivered at `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1459-KOS-EP01-Reference-Architecture-v1.1-Second-Architectural-Review-Semantic-Invariance.md`. **Instrument recommends; it does not accept (R-34)** — the HPA's verdict on it is a separate act.

**Verdict:** ✅ **PASS — CLARIFICATION ONLY.** v1.1 withstands the new evidence unchanged; the Semantic Compiler / SNF / Semantic Invariance Layer are **prepared as Logical-Architecture mechanisms**, none becomes the domain. **No v1.2.**

**Evidence audit (N-0) — the review's first finding.** Most of the cited "new evidence" was already an input to v1.1: the ~74.2% figure **is** the stated reason for the §7.3 non-promotion ruling, the Sanskrit lens already had its architectural home (§7.3 / HPA §1.9), and the SNF source (12:50) **predates v1.1** (14:02). Genuinely post-review (after 14:25): the **false-acceptance finding (~30%, 6/20)**, the **benchmark split**, the **semantic non-collapse** dimension, and the **"enforcement at the boundary, not the kernel"** refinement.

**Decisive argument — Test F (new).** Remove natural language entirely and KnowledgeOS is unharmed (identity · evidence · justification · agency · contradiction · history · states all intact; only *reach* is lost). Therefore **no mechanism whose reason for existing is natural language can be core** — the commission's central question settled: semantic invariance **protects an input boundary around** the core, it is not part of it. Tests A (invariance) and B (compilation) removable; C/D/E already answered in the record and cited, not re-derived.

**Central finding N-1 — the SNF ↔ identity collision.** *A canonical form is still a representation*, so canonical-form equality is a similarity claim in formal dress — and similarity never becomes identity (INV-KOS-IDENTITY-001, Articles 1.2/1.3). SNF-equivalence is a **candidate for the same admitted meaning**, never an identity determination. Prohibition already existed; the mechanism-shaped route into it is new → explicit non-admission note (C-1). **N-3:** invariance without **non-collapse** is a lossy hash (*approved* vs *acknowledged* must not merge) — negative families belong in any future corpus.

**The gap found, and the deliverable that closes it.** v1.1 says what a mechanism may **not do**; it does not say what a mechanism must **declare**. Named for Logical Architecture: the **Expression↔Meaning Port Contract** — 6 obligations, each a render of an existing invariant (candidate-only · justification path · **declared insufficiency/abstention** · never proposes a KnowledgeId · never a scalar for structure · failure→UNKNOWN). A 30% false-accept rate is a mechanism with **no way to abstain**.

**Aggregate review:** all **12 members KEEP** — 0 removals, 0 relocations, 0 additions. Weakest member = **Confidence** (a 74.2%-accurate mechanism would naturally hand over "0.74" — Article 2.3 forbids it) → refinement **R-1**: structured metadata assigned **inside** the boundary; no mechanism score crosses the port as confidence.

**Convergence (N-2 guard):** the new material's "enforcement mechanism at the boundary, not the kernel" **independently re-derives the r2 condition wording** — corroboration that the r2 phrasing was right. But **KOS-EV / KOS-SCB are benchmark names, not architectural components**; and **no event is added** for normalization (mechanism steps are not domain state transitions — the `WisdomDerived` discipline applies).

**Minimal change set (r3 annotations only):** C-1 SNF non-admission · C-2 Meaning = *admitted* content · C-3 EvidenceLinks = references · C-4 Relations by KnowledgeId · C-5 Zero's operational reading (mechanism *"I did not understand"* → UNKNOWN, never ABSENT/FALSE) · R-1 Confidence boundary rule · A-1 falsification results A/B/F recorded · A-2 port-contract forward pointer · A-3 naming/event guard. Invariants **strengthened, none added** (IDENTITY · VERIFICATION · UNKNOWN · DIMENSION touched; seven unaffected).

**Excluded as evidence:** the 74.2%→95% cumulative projection and the "93–95%" claim — a **projection**, refuted inside its own source document by the later pass, and a forbidden action of the v1.1 commission. `20260822-143217-*.md` is **composite**: the later assessment supersedes the earlier projection.

**PROCEDURAL FINDING P-1 — the first review is still open.** The HPA review awaits **confirmation of the applied r2 wording**. This second review does **not** close it. Recommended order: **confirm r2 → rule on this review → apply r3 → open Logical Architecture** with the port contract as its first deliverable; the semantic-invariance experiment runs **in parallel as evidence, never as an architecture layer**.

**Open questions recorded (not decided):** OQ-1 insufficiency vocabulary · OQ-2 may SNF-equivalence be an EvidenceLink for an identity-assignment act · OQ-3 EN/DE sameness = meaning or translation? · **OQ-4 is the real v0.2 experiment authorized (HPA act required)** · OQ-5 should Confidence remain an aggregate member at all.

**Unchanged:** register **25+4** · Constitution **FROZEN** (satisfied, never extended) · research closure **CLOSED** · P4 gate · Semantic Compiler **NOT promoted** · v1.1 **PROPOSED · EVIDENCE-BASED · NON-AUTHORITATIVE**.

## FIRST HPA review CLOSED by confirmation — r2 stands · r3 FROZEN · ⚠️ D-1 wording discrepancy open (2026-08-22, this session)

## KOS Logical Architecture Review 01 — Expression↔Meaning Boundary — DELIVERED (2026-08-22, this session)

**Commission (HPA, 2026-08-22):** review the **Expression↔Meaning Port Contract** as a **DDD Logical-Architecture artifact** — ten challenge questions + the central question (*"does this boundary express a genuine KnowledgeOS domain boundary, or have we accidentally modeled the needs of a semantic compiler as part of the domain?"*), milestone **KOS Logical Architecture Review 01 — Expression↔Meaning Boundary**. Explicitly NOT another SNF simulation · NOT implementation · NOT v0.4.

**Review delivered** at `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1611-KOS-LA-Review-01-Expression-Meaning-Boundary.md` — the **architect-side assessment that feeds the HPA ruling step** (position: Port Contract ← we are here → HPA review/ruling → Logical Architecture → ONLY THEN v0.4).

**VERDICT — PASS.** The boundary expresses a **genuine KnowledgeOS domain boundary**, four load-bearing arguments: (1) the six obligations protect the domain's own constitutional invariants (VERIFICATION · UNKNOWN · IDENTITY · DIMENSION), not compiler capabilities; (2) mechanism-neutrality is structural — §11.1 shows Pāṇinian · dependency · symbolic · LLM · human all feeding the same boundary, so it models none of them; (3) the domain-facing side is the Verification Port — the only admission path (INV-KOS-VERIFICATION-001, D-5); (4) **Test F** is decisive — the core survives natural-language removal, so the core's inbound published language cannot depend on natural language.

**Ten questions answered:** Q1 port = KnowledgeCore's inbound ACL against Expression (correct context) · Q2 "meaning candidate" = integration/published language, never a domain concept (the domain concept is **Meaning**, admitted by ⟨C-2⟩) · Q3 `declared insufficiency` correctly **outside** the core (mechanism self-assessment; negative-only so it can't assert epistemic states; domain handles → UNKNOWN) · Q4 justification path **two-sided** (production mechanism-owned; record + admissibility domain-owned) · Q5 Confidence correctly **aggregate-owned** (⟨R-1⟩; OQ-5 orthogonal) · Q6 EvidenceLink crosses **as a proposal, never a determination** (OQ-2 position affirmed; ruling still the HPA's) · Q7 **no hidden semantic aggregate** (no identity · no protected state · no events ⟨A-3⟩ · r4-4 · Verification Port the only admission path) · Q8 the contract survives SNF disappearing (**SNF-optional by construction** — obligations/answers mechanism-neutral, Q7 intro generalized, DEF-4 encoding-agnostic) · Q9 non-language structured submissions enter **in substance** — Test F warrant; wording to be made explicit (F-3) · Q10 identity · agency · evidence · history · epistemic state all preserved; semantic interpretation stays outside the core.

**Five findings — amendment candidates, all wording/precision, all HPA-gated (no structural defect; contract NOT amended):** F-1 state the two-gate reading (Expression↔Meaning Port = outer published language · Verification Port = only admission path) · F-2 reserve "EvidenceLink" for the domain member — name the crossing artifact "evidence observation / candidate-equality observation" · F-3 make the port explicitly **not a natural-language port** (any representable submission, Test F as warrant) · F-4 pin the preservation altitude (aggregate History at admission, or mechanism-side, never domain state) · F-5 state the two-sided ownership of the justification path.

**Strategic DDD five-question test (Phase 2):** owner = KnowledgeCore (inbound published language) · affected = Expression only (compliance) · Published-Language interaction = yes · ownership change = no · context map change = **no (6 → 6)**. Textbook boundary articulation, not a new sub-domain.

**Boundaries held:** OQ-2/3/5 rulings **the HPA's** · F-1…F-5 **HPA-gated** · v0.4 **not authorized** · OQ-4 **unauthorized** · DEF-4 encoding undecided · DEF-5 no implementation form · Semantic Compiler **NOT promoted** · register **25+4 unchanged** · Constitution **FROZEN** · research **CLOSED** · Logical Architecture **open for the Port Contract only**.

**Next (the HPA's):** review **LA Review 01** + the Port Contract; rule on **OQ-2 · OQ-3 · OQ-5** and the amendment candidates **F-1…F-5**. Then Logical Architecture continues; **SNF v0.4 only after the boundary is ratified**.

## KOS SNF formula classified as a research model · current formula frozen as a research candidate — HPA ACCEPTANCE RECORDED (2026-08-22, this session)

**Act (HPA, verbatim-in-substance):** *"We should not decide the SNF formula before we have finalized the architectural boundary that SNF is supposed to serve … freeze our current SNF formula as a research candidate rather than refine it further right now. The formula can be revisited after the port contract is ratified."*

**Steering instrument:** `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1621-KOS-EP01-SNF-research-model-classification-and-freeze-HPA-acceptance.md`. Position on the chain: r4 steering (commit `564961b3`) → Port Contract DELIVERED → LA Review 01 DELIVERED (PASS, commit `73573e72`) → **HPA accepts + classifies + freezes** → HPA ruling on the Port Contract + LA Review 01 → ratification → Logical Architecture continues → SNF research reopens → competing mechanisms → evidence → possible promotion.

**Three decisions in one act:** (1) **ACCEPT the architectural direction** — *"the port, not SNF, is architectural"*; the question becomes *"what must any semantic mechanism prove before KnowledgeOS is willing to admit its output?"* (2) **CLASSIFY the formula** as **SNF Measurement Research Model v0.x** — research, **not** a *"KnowledgeOS Architecture Formula."* (3) **FREEZE the current formula** as a research candidate — revisit **after the port contract is ratified**. This is **not** *"SNF research is finished"* — it is *"SNF research has reached the correct abstraction boundary."* The two separate questions: **architectural** (now, the Port Contract) vs **research** (later, which mechanism best).

**Competing-mechanism research framing (future · GATED, not authorized):** **SNF-A** symbolic normalization · **SNF-B** graph canonicalization · **SNF-C** Sanskrit/Pāṇinian-inspired semantic roles · **SNF-D** SAIT/NSID · **SNF-E** hybrid — each against the **same port contract** + the **same measurement framework** (convergence · non-collapse · false collapse · stability · abstention). Extends the HPA's Step-5 framing; no premature winner declaration.

**Load-bearing boxed results (HPA-affirmed):** KnowledgeOS ≠ NaturalLanguageSystem (Test F) · KnowledgeOS ≠ SemanticCompiler · SNF ≠ KnowledgeOS. *"That is probably one of the most valuable architectural conclusions we've reached."*

**Reference to the current formula (the HPA's pointer):** `docs/knowledgeos/brainstorming/# Semantic Normal Form (SNF) Formula: Re` (untracked, main checkout) — a research review/improvement analysis of the formula's components; its final discipline statements corroborate the architecture + Port Contract Q8. This act **classifies**, it does not evaluate content.

**Boundaries held:** OQ-2/3/5 rulings **the HPA's** · F-1…F-5 **HPA-gated** · **v0.4 NOT authorized** · **OQ-4 unauthorized** · DEF-4 encoding **undecided** · DEF-5 no implementation form · SNF / Semantic Compiler **NOT promoted** · SNF-A…E comparison **NOT authorized** · register **25+4 unchanged** · Constitution **FROZEN** · research **CLOSED**.

## KOS SNF measurement framework + mechanism competition — REFINED by the HPA — ACCEPTANCE RECORDED (2026-08-22, this session)

**Act (HPA, verbatim-in-substance):** *"Freeze the architecture → ratify the Port Contract → later reopen SNF research → add Coverage/Risk/Calibration to the existing measurement framework → run the A/B/C/D/E competition."* The HPA **keeps the strategic conclusion** (compare competing semantic mechanisms under one invariant measurement framework, not "find the best formula"), **corrects the premature ranking**, and **extends the measurement framework** — all research-design, all GATED, architecture unchanged.

**Steering instrument:** `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1635-KOS-EP01-SNF-measurement-framework-and-competition-refinement-HPA-acceptance.md`. Position: after the SNF classification/freeze (`20260822-1621`, commit `bcdf6053`) and after reading the **uploaded research** — the Senior Mathematical Review (`docs/knowledgeos/brainstorming/20260822-154923-snf-measurement-framework-mathematical-review.md`, untracked, main checkout; status **ARCHITECTURALLY SOUND — MATHEMATICALLY UNDERSPECIFIED**; mechanism/port separation · collision as HARD GATE / safety objective · v0.4 = Bayesian Calibration & Semantic Collision · calibration vocabulary).

**The correction — two moves:** **(1)** `P(M=C) > P(M=E) > P(M=B) > P(M=A) > P(M=D)` is a **pre-experiment prior, not a result**; the HPA would **not** freeze SNF-C as preferred — freeze instead *"the geometry of the problem is multi-objective, constraint-based, and abstention-aware."* The uploaded research: **no single semantic mechanism currently maximizes all criteria.** **(2)** Not a convex optimization problem — the mechanism space is not known convex/continuous/differentiable/vector-space. Formal shape: **constrained multi-objective mechanism-selection** — `M* ∈ {A…G}`, `Φ(M) = (C,NC,FC,T,K,R,A,H,Cal)`; **hard constraints** `FC ≤ ε` · `FalseAccept ≤ ε` (false convergence is constitutionally dangerous); **objectives** `max (C, T, K, Cal)` subject to constraints.

**Measurement vector extended to nine:** `M = (C, NC, FC, T, K, R, A, H, Cal)` — **Coverage K** (admissible-candidate cases / total; the **abstention-inflation guard**) · **Conditional Risk R** (`P(wrong|answered)`; risk–coverage curve; *Good Abstention ≠ High Abstention · Prudence ≠ Inability*) · **Calibration** (confidence ≠ correctness; calibration error · Brier · log loss · reliability curves). **2×2 taxonomy**: correct resolution · **false acceptance (the most dangerous error for KnowledgeOS)** · correct abstention · **blind abstention**.

**SNF-C / SNF-E demoted to hypotheses:** **H-C1** lower false-acceptance at equivalent coverage · **H-C2** better ambiguity detection · **H-C3** lower transformation stability outside native assumptions (*"could reveal its weakness"*) · **H-C4** SNF-E improves coverage preserving C's false-acceptance character. **SNF-E = arbitration hypothesis** — disagreement as information (`D_mech`; agreement → stronger candidate · partial → report divergence · conflict → UNKNOWN); Gaṇeśa + Tarka + Zero consistent. **Semantic adversarial testing** (`FC_adv` · `FalseAccept_adv`). **Pareto-frontier analysis** replaces "find the winner."

**Research program (future · gated):** Phase 1 Port → Phase 2 Measurement (freeze `Φ`) → Phase 3 Mechanisms (SNF-A Symbolic · B Graph · C Kāraka · D SAIT/NSID · E Hybrid · F LLM · G Human) → Phase 4 Benchmark → Phase 5 Pareto. **SNF naming:** mechanism/interface; `SNF(M) → vector`, not `SNF = 0.87`.

**Boundaries held:** ranking = prior, not result · SNF-C/SNF-E = hypotheses · formulas = framing, gated · **v0.4 NOT authorized** · **OQ-4 unauthorized** · A/B/C/D/E/F/G competition **NOT authorized** · **architecture UNCHANGED** (competition behind the port) · OQ-2/3/5 rulings the HPA's · F-1…F-5 HPA-gated · register **25+4 unchanged** · Constitution **FROZEN** · research **CLOSED**.

**Next (the HPA's, unchanged):** review the **Expression↔Meaning Port Contract + LA Review 01**, resolving **OQ-2 · OQ-3 · OQ-5 · F-1…F-5**; then Freeze → ratify → reopen SNF → add Coverage/Risk/Calibration → A/B/C/D/E competition.

## Work — KOS HPA acceptance of the Post-Research Architectural Review (2026-08-22)

**The HPA's ruling (the authoritative act, recorded verbatim-in-substance in the acceptance instrument):** accepts the **NO CHANGE** conclusion — *"the research has become more disciplined, not that the architecture needs to absorb the research."* Affirms the five separations (SNF not core via the disappearance test · no SNF-C/SNF-E choice yet — the earlier "SNF-E constitutional veto ensemble" was too early · the measurement vector `(C,NC,FC,T,K,R,A,H,Cal)` substantially stronger than a single SNF score, with FC/false acceptance as a safety constraint not a positive score · disagreement stays information, never automatically voting — *"C says X, B says X, C says Y"* does not imply *"X wins"* · the KnowledgeAggregate remains the authority boundary — *"probably the most important architectural separation we've established"*).

**The directed correction (applied):** Quality **Gates 2 (Authority Separation)** and **8 (No Premature Promotion)** in the review carried inverted result markings (`❌ No` where the condition was satisfied) — polarity/marking errors, not architectural reasoning. Both corrected to `✅ **Yes**` in the review document §13, with an HPA-directed-correction note added under the heading citing the acceptance instrument. The eight gates now read consistently — all pass.

**The stopping point (HPA):** stop mathematical SNF formula refinement now — enough to define the experimental question, not enough evidence to choose the mechanism. **Do not** modify Reference Architecture v1.1 · **do not** continue searching for the "perfect SNF formula" · **do not** implement the SNF competition yet. The question moved from *"Can we find the formula for SNF?"* to *"Can we construct a semantic mechanism that preserves convergence while preventing collapse, preserves distinctions under transformation, and knows when it does not know?"* — experimentally precise as a **vector**: `Mechanism → (C,NC,FC,T,K,R,A,H,Cal)`, not `Mechanism → SNF = 0.87`. The DDD insight: **the semantic compiler is not the domain — it is an adapter/mechanism boundary around the domain**; SNF-C can be replaced by SNF-E, LLM + SNF-C, or a completely different parser without changing the KnowledgeOS domain model.

**Deliverables:** `docs/knowledgeos/reviews/20260822-1711-KOS-EP01-Post-Research-Architectural-Review-HPA-acceptance.md` (the acceptance instrument) · review document amended (Gates 2 + 8) · CONTEXT.md newest block written (structure verified) · session log appended.

**Boundaries held:** v1.1 **not modified** · SNF refinement **stopped** · competition **not implemented** · no mechanism chosen/promoted · no experiment authorized (v0.4 · OQ-4) · F-1…F-5 remain HPA-gated · register **25+4 unchanged** · Constitution **FROZEN** · research **CLOSED**.

**Next (the HPA's):** the **HPA decision on the Expression↔Meaning Port Contract / Logical Architecture Review 01** (**OQ-2 · OQ-3 · OQ-5 · F-1…F-5**) — the boundary through which any SNF is allowed to participate. After ratification, SNF research deliberately reopens as an empirical mechanism-comparison experiment (Port → Measurement → Mechanisms → Benchmark → Pareto).

## Work — KOS-SNF Research Simulation Phase 1: corrected apparatus + gated 100-case pilot (2026-08-22)

**Commission:** HPA background job — *repair the experimental apparatus before interpreting the measurement*. The predecessor's degenerate C=0/T=0 signal was traced to (1) gold leakage in the evaluator and (2) an inexpressive distance function — not to the mechanisms. Mandate: no architecture modification · research-only apparatus · the four-part separation (surface normalization · semantic candidate generation · semantic equivalence evaluation · identity authority) · d_SNF with decisive inversion/negation separation and explicit unknown-vs-absent role statuses · mechanisms as true black boxes `interpret(expression, context) -> CandidateSet` · SNF-E as a constitutional **veto ensemble** (never a weighted average) · false-consensus experiment · SNF-N null baseline · abstention quality · calibration · **100-case pilot first, then STOP** (no winner, no architecture decision).

**Completed:**
- **RESEARCH BASELINE** — prior SNF/Artha/Sanskrit/EKS brainstorming material read and preserved (not rewritten); HPA's multi-lens framing carried in (Sanskrit/Pāṇinian = the semantic compilation mechanism candidate · SNF-A…E = competing experimental mechanisms · Nyāya/Tarka/Zero/Gödel/Escher = constraints/lenses · KnowledgeOS Kernel = the constitutional epistemic authority independent of all mechanisms; SNF-C = computational formalization of the Pāṇinian lens).
- **KOS-SNF-IR v0.1** — research-only common representation (predicate · arguments[role/entity/status] · negation · modality · temporal · quantification · uncertainty · abstention · provenance); role statuses EXPRESSED/UNKNOWN/NOT_EXPRESSED/AMBIGUOUS/NOT_LICENSED (**unknown ≠ absent ≠ present**); identity authority explicitly absent.
- **d_SNF two-tier pre-metric** — Tier 1 decisive (predicate 0.65 · negation 0.65 · entity mismatch 0.65 · argument inversion +0.20), Tier 2 graded (status-pair weights 0.15–0.50, role blend 0.65·max+0.35·mean), clamped [0,1]; triangle inequality **not** guaranteed — documented honestly. **Sanity gate 8/8 PASS** (A identical 0.000 · B paraphrase 0.000 · C active/passive 0.000 · D inversion 0.850 · E negation 0.650 · F different predicate 0.650 · G ambiguous 0.430 · H incomplete 0.330) — a hard gate in the runner that refuses to run mechanisms if it fails.
- **Six black-box mechanisms** SNF-A/B/C/D/E/N under one identical contract; SNF-C = kāraka role lens (kartṛ/karman/karaṇa/sampradāna/adhikaraṇa/apādāna); SNF-E = veto/agreement ensemble with configurable arbitration rule (`abstain_conflict` default), promotes least-divergent member else partial else ABSTAIN — no weighted average anywhere; SNF-N = most-common-candidate prior, never abstains.
- **Corpus** `KOS-SNF-pilot-100.json` — 100 hand-written cases (20 each of equivalent/distinct/ambiguous/transformation/adversarial); gold lives only in the corpus and is never given to a mechanism.
- **Runner** `scripts/snf-research/run-pilot.py` — deterministic, seeded (`20260822`), gated, writes results + metrics JSONs; **byte-reproducible** (two subprocess runs → identical SHA-256: results `1f639703…`, metrics `7170e2cd…`).
- **Pilot run + 18-section report** `KOS-SNF-RESEARCH-REFINEMENT-001.md` ending in the **PILOT COMPLETE** block (Evidence · Conclusion VALID(apparatus)/REQUIRES REFINEMENT(question) · Next research question). Index entry appended (`00_INDEX.md` — experiment record, frozen classifier untouched).

**Decisions (research, never architecture):** three apparatus defects fixed **before** interpreting — (a) SNF-D registry check now compares canonical identifiers (`NOUNS[t]`) not surface tokens, fixing the plural-abstention defect; (b) warranted-abstention accounting uses the shared `tokenize()`, fixing the punctuation-suffix defect; (c) consensus gold verdict uses the corpus's **declared relation**, not a d_snf inference, fixing presence-trap mis-classification. No gold leakage (mechanisms receive only `(expression, context)`). SNF-E veto-ensemble by construction. **Consensus is measured, never trusted.** `P(θ|E,C)` preserved as epistemic uncertainty and explicitly ≠ `Identity(E,θ)`.

**Results (headline):** pairwise consensus false-rate 0.319 — 0% on same-meaning categories, 100% on distinct/ambiguous/adversarial; strict consensus correctness 0.60 (5 events, 2 false, both ambiguous over-commitment); only SNF-D abstains (precision 1.0, recall 0.60); SNF-N at chance (C=3/80, R=0.038, Brier 0.25) → **abstention and convergence carry information**; SNF-E worse-calibrated than its members (Brier 0.052 vs A 0.0225) — arbitration confidence is not a probability; overcommitment is the dominant failure mode on ambiguous cases (B 20/20 · D 17/20 · A/C/E 14/20); inter-mechanism agreement κ=0.259.

**Boundaries held:** no architecture modification · no winner selected · no Port Contract / Governance / Constitution / identity-authority change · no SNF formula finalized · SNF stays a candidate mechanism set behind the semantic-interpretation boundary · register 25+4 unchanged · Constitution FROZEN · STOP honored.

**Next (deferred — NOT authorized here):** 1,000-case competition across categories · per-rule SNF-E arbitration sweep · presence-sensitivity d_SNF variant. The next research question is stated verbatim in report §18.

## KOS-SNF P5 — Semantic Competition Refinement — EXECUTED, COMPLETE (2026-08-22, this session)

**Act:** HPA commissioned P5 (23 sections, 16-step order); plan produced under EP-01 and **approved with amendments** — Q-1 **OQ-4 remains unauthorized** (toy-world simulation only, no real-language claims) · Q-2 **honest reduction accepted** (translation/legal/narrative/abstract/institutional/procedural/technical-ontology/metaphor-pragmatics excluded and recorded, not faked) · Q-3 **`DOMAIN_CONDITIONAL` deferred** (no fake domain taxonomy) · Q-4 **templates + independently declared hand-written gold** · **D-3 strengthened** to the four-way distinction. Plan: `docs/plans/20260822-1856-kos-snf-p5-semantic-competition-refinement-plan.md`. Report: `docs/knowledgeos/brainstorming/KOS-SNF-RESEARCH-P5-COMPETITION-001.md`.

**TDD honored:** 51 tests written RED first (import errors + a genuine failure), then implementation, then GREEN.

**Nine defects repaired:** D-1 ceiling (1,100-case corpus, 12 families, max share 10.9%) · D-2 presence insensitivity + D-3 UNKNOWN/NOT_EXPRESSED collapse (**category floors** in `d_SNF v0.2`; v0.1 retained as A/B baseline) · D-4 arbitration (six behaviourally **distinct** policies) · D-5 calibration confound (`calibration_at_coverage`) · D-6 abstention cost (four-way taxonomy; false acceptance as a **constraint**, never a score) · D-7 hard-coded sizes (all denominators derived) · D-8 no test suite · D-9 no intervals (seeded bootstrap CIs). **Plus a defect the tests found: the Phase-1 claim `d(x,x)=0` was FALSE for UNKNOWN-bearing IRs (measured 0.124)** — reflexivity restored in v0.2.

**Established by evidence (toy world, one seed):** (1) the Phase-1 ceiling was a corpus artifact — same producers drop R 1.000 → 0.688; (2) v0.1 collapsed EXPRESSED/NOT_EXPRESSED (0.330) and NOT_EXPRESSED/UNKNOWN (0.247) below τ=0.40; v0.2 separates all four states; (3) **the collapse had teeth** — under v0.1 producers scored CORRECT on **45/90 false-consensus traps**, under v0.2 **0/90**; (4) **three of six named arbitration rules were behavioural aliases** in Phase-1 code; (5) arbitration is a real risk–coverage dial (UNANIMOUS: wrong pairs 290→200, abstention 0.046→0.196, FA rate 0.706→0.529); (6) **0.587 of agreeing mechanism-pairs agree on a WRONG reading**; (7) **SNF-D's Phase-1 calibration advantage was a selection effect** — it reverses under coverage matching (A/B/C 0.24→0.17 Brier, D unchanged at 0.248); (8) **no producer represents the UNKNOWN vs NOT_EXPRESSED distinction at all** (110/110 failures, every producer); (9) **SNF-C's Pāṇinian analysis shows exactly one measurable advantage** — `role_omission_ambiguity` 60/60 handled where A/B/D/E fail 60/60 — and none elsewhere; (10) all producers abstain 50/50 on out-of-world content but commit on genuinely ambiguous cases — refusing an unknown *word* is easy, refusing an underdetermined *reading* is not; (11) byte-reproducible (sweep digest `9ce9d670cb47` identical across runs).

**Not established:** which SNF formulation is correct (the **Pareto frontier changes with the arbitration policy**, so "which is best" is not well-posed until a policy is fixed) · anything about natural language · that v0.2 is *correct* (a repaired pre-metric, no triangle inequality claimed, floors chosen not derived) · modality separation (declared DISTINCT but graded 0.10 — AH-5) · abstention pricing.

**Five architectural hypotheses recorded and STOPPED at "HPA DECISION REQUIRED":** AH-1 the Port Contract's *declared insufficiency* may need to distinguish "no filler" from "filler unknown" · AH-2 the 0.587 false-agreement rate corroborates (never extends) INV-KOS-IDENTITY-001 · AH-3 registry ignorance vs semantic underdetermination may be distinct port declarations · AH-4 a promotion argument must name its metric version · AH-5 whether *asserted* vs *possible* is a boundary or a degree is a modelling question research cannot settle.

**⛔ Boundary held:** v1.1 r3 **unmodified** · Constitution **FROZEN** · KnowledgeAggregate authority **unchanged** · Port Contract **unamended** · no winner · no composite authority score · no mechanism promoted · no accuracy projection · **OQ-4 still unauthorized** · register **25+4 unchanged** · research/production separation kept (only `scripts/snf-research/` + `docs/knowledgeos/brainstorming/` touched).

**Next:** HPA ruling on AH-1…AH-5 (AH-1 and AH-3 are inputs to the Port Contract stage, not amendments of it), plus the still-open OQ-2 · OQ-3 · OQ-5 · F-1…F-5.

### EP-02 completion review — independent verification (this session, post-approval receipt)

The executed P5 (commit `92d7e5eb`) was verified by **re-running from committed code** — not by trusting the report's self-claims (EP-02 / R-34). Every check passed:

- **Tests:** `run-tests.py` → **51 tests OK** (alias-pinning, deferred-policy, derived-denominator, no-composite, bootstrap-CI all present).
- **d_SNF V02 gate:** **14/14 PASS** — all four D-3 states separated 0.45–1.00; pair K NOT_EXPRESSED vs UNKNOWN = 0.450 ≥ τ 0.40. V01 retained (reflexivity defect d(x,x)=0.124 preserved as baseline; V02 = 0.000).
- **Corpus:** 1,100 cases · 12 families · digest `e9fb867903fb` · every case declares relation + non-empty rationale · `corpus_1000.py` imports stdlib + `ir` only (Q-4 gold never producer-generated).
- **Policies:** exactly six; `DOMAIN_CONDITIONAL` in `POLICY_DEFERRED` and rejected by `SNFE` (ValueError) — Q-3 honoured.
- **Reproducibility:** two full competition runs → sweep digest **`9ce9d670cb47` identical**, matching the committed artifact; only `meta.elapsed_s` differs.
